## §12.2.1 Packet Loss Handling (Lossy Transports)

| Unit | Kind | Level | Scenario | Lock | Python | Rust |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RK-12.2.1-001 | behavior | should | untested: Whether a backend conceals loss, which no runner reaches: a scenario feeds PCM frames, with no RTP sequence numbers. roomkit's SIP backend enables concealment by default and hands it to its RTP dependency, tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_default_enabled, ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_reaches_call_session and ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_disabled_reaches_call_session; a provider proves its mapping of its vendor's wire in its own tests, outside roomkit's conformance, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.1-005 | behavior | must | untested: What any backend implementing PLC must do on its own transport, which no runner reaches: a scenario feeds PCM frames, with no RTP sequence numbers and no jitter buffer. roomkit's SIP backend hands detection and concealment to its RTP dependency, which tests them (tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_reaches_call_session and ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_disabled_reaches_call_session check the hand-over); a provider proves its mapping of its vendor's wire in its own tests, outside roomkit's conformance, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.1-006 | behavior | must | untested: What any backend implementing PLC must do on its own transport, which no runner reaches: a scenario feeds PCM frames, with no RTP timestamps or sequence numbers. roomkit's SIP backend hands detection and concealment to its RTP dependency, which tests them (tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_reaches_call_session and ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_disabled_reaches_call_session check the hand-over); a provider proves its mapping of its vendor's wire in its own tests, outside roomkit's conformance, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.1-007 | behavior | must | untested: What any backend implementing PLC must do on its own transport, which no runner reaches: a scenario feeds PCM frames, with no RFC 4733 events in an RTP stream. roomkit's SIP backend hands detection and concealment to its RTP dependency, which tests them (tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_reaches_call_session and ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_disabled_reaches_call_session check the hand-over); a provider proves its mapping of its vendor's wire in its own tests, outside roomkit's conformance, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.1-008 | behavior | must | untested: What any backend implementing PLC must do on its own transport, which no runner reaches: a scenario feeds PCM frames and loses no packet. roomkit's SIP backend hands detection and concealment to its RTP dependency, which tests them (tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_reaches_call_session and ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_disabled_reaches_call_session check the hand-over); a provider proves its mapping of its vendor's wire in its own tests, outside roomkit's conformance, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.1-009 | behavior | should | untested: What a backend implementing PLC should do with its codec, which no runner reaches: a scenario feeds PCM frames and negotiates no codec. roomkit's SIP backend hands detection and concealment to its RTP dependency, which tests them (tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_reaches_call_session and ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_disabled_reaches_call_session check the hand-over); a provider proves its mapping of its vendor's wire in its own tests, outside roomkit's conformance, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.2.1-010 | behavior | must | untested: What any backend implementing PLC must do with a codec that has no native concealment, which no runner reaches: a scenario feeds PCM frames and negotiates no codec. roomkit's SIP backend hands detection and concealment to its RTP dependency, which tests them (tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_reaches_call_session and ::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_plc_disabled_reaches_call_session check the hand-over); a provider proves its mapping of its vendor's wire in its own tests, outside roomkit's conformance, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |

| RK-12.2.1-012 | behavior | must | untested: What any backend implementing PLC must expose, which no runner reaches: the runner's scripted backend loses no packet. roomkit's SIP backend syncs concealed_frames into its per-session audio stats, tests/voice/backends/test_sip.py::TestPacketLossConcealment::test_concealed_frames_synced_at_cleanup, but on private state, with packets_lost only logged, a suspect (RMK-2024); a provider proves its mapping of its vendor's wire in its own tests, outside roomkit's conformance, and roomkit-rs has no voice backend (O1). | | | |

| RK-12.10.1-002 | behavior | must | untested: Whether media between human participants crosses RoomKit is the SFU's routing, which no step reads: Python's ConferenceChannel publishes only its bot's own speech, on one bot session, tests/conference/test_conference_outbound.py::TestSingleBotTrack::test_everything_is_published_on_one_bot_session and ::test_without_tts_nothing_is_published, and holds no route from one participant's track to another, by review of src/roomkit/channels/conference.py; roomkit-rs has no conference (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.10.1-003 | behavior | must | decided gap: step, RMK-2070: step: conference_already_attached error name: a second conference channel attached to a room already holding one, refused under a name no runner error table holds. | | | |

| RK-12.10.1-005 | behavior | must | decided gap: step, RMK-2070: step: conference.feed: speech on a participant's track stored as a RoomEvent of the room. | | | |
| RK-12.10.1-006 | behavior | must | decided gap: step, RMK-2070: step: conference.feed: a transcription through BEFORE_BROADCAST, the room's permissions and the broadcast to its other channels, as any inbound event. | | | |
| RK-12.10.1-007 | behavior | must | untested: What passes between a client and its SFU is no runner's to read: Python's ConferenceChannel only hands back the credential its backend minted, tests/conference/test_conference_channel.py::TestAccessMinting::test_channel_mints_access_with_its_default_grants, and takes presence from the backend's callbacks, ::TestParticipantRecords::test_joining_participant_becomes_a_room_participant; that it proxies no signalling or media is by review of src/roomkit/channels/; roomkit-rs has no conference (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.10.1-008 | behavior | must | decided gap: step, RMK-2070: step: conference.read: one join_as_bot per conference, its subscriptions and the bot's publications on that one session. | | | |
| RK-12.10.1-009 | behavior | must | untested: What any conference backend must deliver, which no runner reaches (the runner's scripted SFU is told the publisher): roomkit's mock and contract, tests/conference/test_mock_backend.py::TestPublishing::test_tracks_carry_room_and_publisher and tests/conference/test_backend_abc.py::TestCallbackFanout::test_audio_frames_are_attributable_to_participant_and_room; a provider proves its mapping of its vendor's wire in its own tests, outside roomkit's conformance, and roomkit-rs has no conference (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.10.1-010 | behavior | must | decided gap: step, RMK-2070: step: conference.feed: two participants speaking on their own tracks, each transcription attributed to its track's publisher with no diarization stage. | | | |
| RK-12.10.1-011 | behavior | must | untested: The backend boundary is an interface no step reads: Python's ConferenceBackend delivers AudioFrames and takes credentials the framework hands on unread, pinned in tests/public_api_signatures.json and checked by tests/test_public_api.py::TestPublicAPI::test_no_signature_change_breaks_a_caller (roomkit.ConferenceBackend, roomkit.ConferenceAccess); the decoding a backend owns, tests/conference/test_mock_injection.py::TestHeterogeneousFormats::test_a_track_carries_the_format_its_publisher_negotiated; a provider proves its mapping of its vendor's wire in its own tests, outside roomkit's conformance, and roomkit-rs has no conference (O1). | | | |
| RK-12.10.1-012 | behavior | must | untested: An interface property no step reads: none of Python's conference interfaces (roomkit.ConferenceBackend, roomkit.ConferenceChannel, the conference models) takes SDP, ICE, a codec, a simulcast layer or a bitrate, pinned in tests/public_api_signatures.json and checked by tests/test_public_api.py::TestPublicAPI::test_no_signature_change_breaks_a_caller; by review; roomkit-rs has no conference (O1). | | | |

| RK-12.10.4-031 | behavior | must | untested: No scenario can tell a close that waits for an operation in flight from one that does not. The operation ends only once a step lets it go, and no step can know that the close has reached its wait: the close shows nothing of its wait until the store's release that follows it. A step that lets the operation go before the close reaches its wait shows nothing, and one that lets it go after can only wait on the clock (the last unwind's second). The operations it covers are those the framework and its own channels start of their own accord (a conference's roster writes and reads, a realtime session's reads for its tool calls, the room context read for one of the framework's announcements), and Python's conference channel waits for its callbacks first, in its own close, on a budget of its own (5 s). Python: tests/conference/test_conference_races.py::TestARosterWriteOutlivingTheChannel::test_the_store_is_not_released_under_a_write_it_is_running asserts that the close does not return while a roster write is in the store, and that neither the store nor the lock manager is used once closed, and ::test_a_wedged_store_does_not_hold_the_bot_in_the_conference that the bot leaves and the backend closes first; tests/conference/test_conference_races.py::TestOneChannelsStoreIsNotAnothersMedia::test_the_store_outlives_a_read_in_flight the same for a read. roomkit-rs has no channel that starts an operation of its own accord (no conference, no realtime voice): its close unwinds the work it cut, then seals the kit and closes the store, after its channels: crates/roomkit/tests/close/shutdown.rs (the_close_closes_the_store_once_after_the_channels). | | | |
| RK-12.10.4-032 | behavior | must | `kit-close/a-channel-s-write-still-in-the-store-past-the-close-s-unwind-fails-under-it` | pinned | memory passed | memory skipped |
| RK-12.10.4-033 | behavior | must | untested: That work resuming once the resources are sealed is refused with an error naming the shutdown is seen by no step. The work it covers is a callback the framework's own channel runs of its own accord (a conference's participant callback, parked in its backend), whose refusal reaches no caller. In Python the conference channel's own barrier, raised by its close, stops such a callback before it asks for the store, so a scenario sees nothing written with the framework's seal or without it (probed with a join parked across the close, roomkit d6a3d9056), and the close it parks across takes the channel's budgets (10.5 s), past the 10 s a step may take. Python: tests/conference/test_conference_races.py::TestACallbackResumingAfterTheShutdown::test_no_lease_is_granted_once_the_shutdown_has_sealed asserts that a hold on the store asked once the shutdown sealed it is refused with a KitClosedError naming the close, and ::test_a_join_abandoned_by_the_budget_reads_and_writes_nothing that the resumed callback reads and writes nothing. roomkit-rs has no channel that runs callbacks of its own accord; its own work refuses to write once the kit is sealed (RoomKitError::KitClosed, kit/channel_state.rs keep_channel_state, driver run_own), and work its close spared delivers nothing then: crates/roomkit/tests/close/doors.rs (a_spared_run_delivers_nothing_once_the_kit_is_sealed). | | | |
